\displaystyle{=}\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{L_{1}\Delta}{T}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2q}}\sqrt{\frac{\delta\Delta}{T}}+\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]}{\alpha T}+\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{L_{1}\Delta}{T}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2q}}\sqrt{\frac{\delta\Delta}{T}}+\frac{\varepsilon}{\alpha T}+\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{L_{1}\Delta}{T}}+\left[\sqrt{\frac{\delta\Delta}{T}}+\alpha_{\textnormal{eff}}^{-\frac{1}{2q}}\sqrt{\frac{\delta\Delta}{T}}\right]+\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\alpha_{\textnormal{eff}}^{-1}+\sigma_{1}\alpha_{\textnormal{eff}}^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\right)

\displaystyle{=}\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{L_{1}\Delta}{T}}+\sqrt{\frac{\delta\Delta}{T}}+\sigma_{1}\left(\frac{\delta\Delta}{\sigma_{1}^{2}T}\right)^{\frac{q(p-1)}{p(2q+1)-2q}}+\sigma_{1}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{\sigma_{1}T}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p-1}}\right),

(82)

where in (
) we use the choice of
\alpha_{\textnormal{eff}}
from (
D.3
) since

\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\alpha_{\textnormal{eff}}^{-1}\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}T}{\varepsilon}\right)^{\frac{p}{2p-1}}=\sigma_{1}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{\sigma_{1}T}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p-1}},

and

\sigma_{1}\alpha_{\textnormal{eff}}^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\leq\sigma_{1}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{\sigma_{1}T}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p-1}}+\sigma_{1}\left(\frac{\delta\Delta}{\sigma_{1}^{2}T}\right)^{\frac{q(p-1)}{p(2q+1)-2q}}.

Finally, from the bound (
82
) we deduce that the sample complexity of
Algorithm

1
is exactly

\mathcal{O}\left(\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\varepsilon}\right)^{\frac{p}{p-1}}+\frac{(L_{1}+\delta)\Delta}{\varepsilon^{2}}+\frac{\delta\Delta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\varepsilon}\right)^{\frac{p}{q(p-1)}}\right),

as claimed, and, combining
Algorithm

1
with
NSGD-Mom
is enough to match our lower bound from
Theorem

3.2
.
∎

D.4
Proof of
Theorem

4.3

Theorem
4.3
.

Under
2.1
,
2.3
and
2.6
, let the initial gradient estimate
g_{0}=\nabla f(x_{0},\xi_{0})
, let the stepsize
\gamma=\sqrt{\frac{\Delta\alpha}{\bar{L}T}}
, the momentum parameter
\alpha=T^{-\frac{1}{2}}\in\mathopen{(}0\mathclose{},1\mathclose{]}
. Then,
Algorithm

1
guarantees the bound

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|\right]=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{T^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}}+\frac{\sqrt{\bar{L}\Delta}}{T^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}}}\right).

Proof.

Notice that the bound (
75
) holds for any choice of the parameters
\gamma>0
,
\alpha\in\mathopen{(}0\mathclose{},1\mathclose{]}
and
g_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Hence, for our particular choice
g_{0}=\nabla f(x_{0},\xi_{0})
,
\gamma=\sqrt{\frac{\Delta\alpha}{\bar{L}T}}
and
\alpha=T^{-\frac{1}{2}}
we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|\right]

\displaystyle{=}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\Delta}{\gamma T}+\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]}{\alpha T}+\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}+\gamma\bar{L}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\bar{L}\Delta}{T}}+\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]}{T^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}}+\frac{\sigma_{1}}{T^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}}+\alpha^{-\frac{2-q}{2q}}\sqrt{\frac{\bar{L}\Delta}{T}}\right)

\displaystyle{=}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\bar{L}\Delta}}{T^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}}}+\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]}{T^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}}+\frac{\sigma_{1}}{T^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}}\right)

\displaystyle{=}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{T^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}}+\frac{\sigma_{1}}{T^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}}+\frac{\sqrt{\bar{L}\Delta}}{T^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{T^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}}+\frac{\sqrt{\bar{L}\Delta}}{T^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}}}\right),

where in (
) we use
\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\geq\alpha^{-\frac{2-q}{2q}}
since
\alpha\in\mathopen{(}0\mathclose{},1\mathclose{]}
and
\frac{2-q}{2q}\leq\frac{1}{2}
. In (
) we use the definition of
\hat{e}_{0}
and
g_{0}
, i.e.,

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x_{0},\xi_{0})-\nabla F(x_{0})\right\|\right]\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Lem.}\!~\text{\ref{lem:jensen-inequality}}}}{\leq}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x_{0},\xi_{0})-\nabla F(x_{0})\right\|^{p}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Ass.}\!~\text{\ref{ass:p-bounded-central-moment-gradient}}}}{\leq}\sigma_{1}.

This achieves the proof of the theorem.
∎

D.5
Proof of
Theorem

4.4

Theorem
4.4
.

Under
2.1
,
2.2
,
2.3
and
2.7
, let the initial gradient estimate
g_{0}=\nabla f(x_{0},\xi_{0})
, let the stepsize
\gamma=\min\left\{\sqrt{\frac{\Delta}{L_{1}T}},\sqrt{\frac{\Delta\alpha}{\delta T}}\right\}
, the momentum parameter
\alpha=T^{-\frac{1}{2}}\in\mathopen{(}0\mathclose{},1\mathclose{]}
. Then,
Algorithm

1
guarantees guarantees the bound

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|\right]=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{T^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}}+\frac{\sqrt{\delta\Delta}}{T^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}}}+\frac{\sqrt{L_{1}\Delta}}{T^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}}\right).

Proof.

As in the previous theorem, note that the bound (
D.3
) holds for any choice of the parameters
\gamma>0
,
\alpha\in\mathopen{(}0\mathclose{},1\mathclose{]}
and
g_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Hence, for our particular choice
g_{0}=\nabla f(x_{0},\xi_{0})
,
\gamma=\min\left\{\sqrt{\frac{\Delta}{L_{1}T}},\sqrt{\frac{\Delta\alpha}{\delta T}}\right\}
and
\alpha=T^{-\frac{1}{2}}
we obtain, as in the previous proof

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|\right]

\displaystyle{=}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\Delta}{\gamma T}+\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]}{\alpha T}+\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}+\gamma\delta\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}+\gamma L_{1}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{L_{1}\Delta}{T}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\delta\Delta}{T}}+\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]}{T^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}}+\frac{\sigma_{1}}{T^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}}+\alpha^{-\frac{2-q}{2q}}\sqrt{\frac{\delta\Delta}{T}}\right)

\displaystyle{=}\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{L_{1}\Delta}{T}}+\frac{\sqrt{\delta\Delta}}{T^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}}}+\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]}{T^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}}+\frac{\sigma_{1}}{T^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}}\right)

\displaystyle{=}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{T^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}}+\frac{\sigma_{1}}{T^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}}+\frac{\sqrt{\delta\Delta}}{T^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{L_{1}\Delta}{T}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{T^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}}+\frac{\sqrt{\delta\Delta}}{T^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}}}+\sqrt{\frac{L_{1}\Delta}{T}}\right),

where in (
) we use
\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\geq\alpha^{-\frac{2-q}{2q}}
since
\alpha\in\mathopen{(}0\mathclose{},1\mathclose{]}
and
\frac{2-q}{2q}\leq\frac{1}{2}
. In (
) we use, as before, the bound (
D.5
).

This achieves the proof of the theorem.
∎

Appendix E
Missing Proofs in Section
5

E.1
Auxiliary Lemmas

Some of the auxiliary lemmas needed in this section (
Lemmas

D.2
and
D.4
) have already been established in the previous section.

Lemma E.1

(Bounding the Error Term)
.

Under
2.3
,
2.5
and
2.4
, for all
t\in\left\{0,\ldots,T-1\right\}
we have

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{t}\right\|\right]\leq(1-\alpha)^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]+2\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}+2\gamma\sigma_{2}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}+4\gamma\min\left\{L_{1},\gamma L_{2}\right\}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}},

where
\hat{e}_{t}:=g_{t}-\nabla F(x_{t})
.

Proof.

By the update rule of the gradient estimator in
Algorithm

2
(line 9) we have, for all
t\in\left\{1,\ldots,T-1\right\}

\displaystyle\hat{e}_{t}:\!

\displaystyle=g_{t}-\nabla F(x_{t})

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha)(g_{t-1}+\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{t},\hat{\xi}_{t})(x_{t}-x_{t-1})+\alpha\nabla f(x_{t},\xi_{t})-\nabla F(x_{t})

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha)(g_{t-1}-\nabla F(x_{t-1}))\begin{aligned} &+\alpha\left(\nabla f(x_{t},\xi_{t})-\nabla F(x_{t})\right)\\
&+(1-\alpha)\left(\nabla F(x_{t-1})-\nabla F(x_{t})-\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{t},\hat{\xi}_{t})(x_{t-1}-x_{t})\right)\end{aligned}

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha)(g_{t-1}-\nabla F(x_{t-1}))\begin{aligned} &+\alpha\left(\nabla f(x_{t},\xi_{t})-\nabla F(x_{t})\right)\\
&+(1-\alpha)\left(\nabla F(x_{t-1})-\nabla F(x_{t})-\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})(x_{t-1}-x_{t})\right)\\
&+(1-\alpha)\left(\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})(x_{t-1}-x_{t})-\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{t},\hat{\xi}_{t})(x_{t-1}-x_{t})\right)\end{aligned}

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha)\hat{e}_{t-1}+\alpha e_{t}+(1-\alpha)\hat{R}_{t}+(1-\alpha)\hat{S}_{t},

(84)

where we let
e_{t}:=\nabla f(x_{t},\xi_{t})-\nabla F(x_{t})
and

\displaystyle\hat{R}_{t}:\!

\displaystyle=\nabla F(x_{t-1})-\nabla F(x_{t})-\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})(x_{t-1}-x_{t}),

(85)

\displaystyle\hat{S}_{t}:\!

\displaystyle=\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})(x_{t-1}-x_{t})-\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{t},\hat{\xi}_{t})(x_{t-1}-x_{t}).

(86)

Notably, it is worth mentioning that
(e_{t})_{t\geq 0}
,
(\hat{R}_{t})_{t\geq 0}
and
(\hat{S}_{t})_{t\geq 0}
are all martingale difference sequence with respect to the filtration
(\mathcal{F}_{t})_{t\geq 0}
where
\mathcal{F}_{t}:=\sigma(g_{0},(\xi_{1},\hat{\xi}_{1},q_{1}),\ldots,(\xi_{t},\hat{\xi}_{t},q_{t}))
. Effectively, for
\hat{R}_{t}
we have

{\mathbb{E}}_{q_{t}}\left[F(\hat{x}_{t})(x_{t}-x_{t-1})\right]=\int_{0}^{1}\nabla^{2}F(sx_{t}+(1-s)x_{t-1})(x_{t}-x_{t-1})\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}s=\nabla F(x_{t})-\nabla F(x_{t-1}).

Then, unrolling the recursion (
84
) gives

\hat{e}_{t}=(1-\alpha)^{t}\hat{e}_{0}+\alpha\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-j-1}e_{j+1}+\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\hat{R}_{j+1}+\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\hat{S}_{j+1},

and taking the norm followed by the total expectation yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{t}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\alpha)^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]+\alpha\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-j-1}e_{j+1}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle+(1-\alpha)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-j-1}\hat{R}_{j+1}\right\|\right]+(1-\alpha)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-j-1}\hat{S}_{j+1}\right\|\right].

We now need to upper bound the last three terms of the previous inequality. For the first term, using Jensen’s inequality (
Lemma

H.9
) as we did before in
Lemma

D.5
(see (
70
)) we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-j-1}e_{j+1}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}2\sigma_{1}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{p}}.

(88)

Then, for the second term we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-j-1}\hat{R}_{j+1}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-j-1}\hat{R}_{j+1}\right\|^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle{\leq}\left(2\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{2(t-j-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{R}_{j+1}\right\|^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

(89)

and, the variance of
\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})(x_{t}-x_{t-1})
can be bounded in two different ways. First, by using
2.2
and Jensen’s inequality we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{R}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle{=}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla F(x_{t-1})-\nabla F(x_{t})-\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})(x_{t-1}-x_{t})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})-\nabla F(x_{t-1})\right\|^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})(x_{t}-x_{t-1})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}2L_{1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x_{t}-x_{t-1}\right\|^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})\right\|_{\text{op}}^{2}\cdot\left\|x_{t}-x_{t-1}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}4L_{1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x_{t}-x_{t-1}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=4\gamma^{2}L_{1}^{2},

(90)

and, using the connection between
\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})(x_{t}-x_{t-1})
and
\nabla F(x_{t})-\nabla F(x_{t-1})
as displayed in (
E.1
) we also have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{R}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle{=}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla F(x_{t-1})-\nabla F(x_{t})-\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})(x_{t-1}-x_{t})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\int_{0}^{1}\left(\nabla^{2}F(x_{t-1}+s(x_{t}-x_{t-1}))-\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})\right)(x_{t}-x_{t-1})\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}s\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{1}\left\|\left(\nabla^{2}F(x_{t-1}+s(x_{t}-x_{t-1}))-\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})\right)(x_{t}-x_{t-1})\right\|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}s\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{1}\left\|\nabla^{2}F(x_{t-1}+s(x_{t}-x_{t-1}))-\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})\right\|_{\text{op}}\cdot\left\|x_{t}-x_{t-1}\right\|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}s\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\gamma^{2}\,\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{1}\left\|\nabla^{2}F(x_{t-1}+s(x_{t}-x_{t-1}))-\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})\right\|_{\text{op}}\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}s\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}\gamma^{2}L_{2}^{2}\,\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{1}\left\|s(x_{t}-\hat{x}_{t})+(1-s)(x_{t-1}-\hat{x}_{t})\right\|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}s\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}\gamma^{2}L_{2}^{2}\,\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{1}s\left\|x_{t}-\hat{x}_{t}\right\|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}s+\int_{0}^{1}(1-s)\left\|x_{t-1}-\hat{x}_{t}\right\|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}s\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma^{2}L_{2}^{2}}{4}\,\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|x_{t}-\hat{x}_{t}\right\|+\left\|x_{t-1}-\hat{x}_{t}\right\|\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle{=}\frac{\gamma^{2}L_{2}^{2}}{4}\,\mathbb{E}\left[\left((1-q_{t})\left\|x_{t}-x_{t-1}\right\|+q_{t}\left\|x_{t}-x_{t-1}\right\|\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma^{4}L_{2}^{2}}{4},

(91)

where in (
) and (
) we use the triangle inequality. In (
) we use the definition of
\hat{x}_{t}
, i.e.,
\hat{x}_{t}:=q_{t}x_{t}+(1-q_{t})x_{t-1}
. It is worth mentioning that the bounds (
90
) and (
91
) holds without the expectation
\mathbb{E}\left[\cdot\right]
.

Thus, using (
94
), (
91
) we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-j-1}\hat{R}_{j+1}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}4\gamma\min\left\{L_{1},\gamma L_{2}\right\}\left(\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{2(t-j-1)}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\gamma\min\left\{L_{1},\gamma L_{2}\right\}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

(92)

Finally, for the last term, we can write

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-j-1}\hat{S}_{j+1}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-j-1}\hat{S}_{j+1}\right\|^{q}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}

\displaystyle{\leq}\left(2\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{q(t-j-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{S}_{j+1}\right\|^{q}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{q}},

(93)

and, using
2.5
we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{S}_{j+1}\right\|^{q}\right]

\displaystyle{=}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})(x_{t-1}-x_{t})-\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{t},\hat{\xi}_{t})(x_{t-1}-x_{t})\right\|^{q}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{t})-\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{t},\hat{\xi}_{t})\right\|_{\text{op}}^{q}\cdot\left\|x_{t}-x_{t-1}\right\|^{q}\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q},

(94)

thus, using (
94
) and the fact that
2^{\frac{1}{q}}\leq 2
we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-j-1}\hat{S}_{j+1}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}2\gamma\sigma_{2}\left(\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}(1-\alpha)^{q(t-j-1)}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\gamma\sigma_{2}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}.

(95)

Then, combining the bounds (
88
), (
92
) and (
95
) we have

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{t}\right\|\right]\leq(1-\alpha)^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]+2\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}+2\gamma\sigma_{2}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}+4\gamma\min\left\{L_{1},\gamma L_{2}\right\}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}},

which achieves the proof of the lemma.
∎

E.2
Proof of
Theorem

5.1

Thanks to
Lemmas

D.4
and
E.1
we can now establish the convergence analysis (in expectation) of
Algorithm

2
.

Theorem
5.1
.

Under
2.1
,
2.2
,
2.4
,
2.3
and
2.5
, let the initial gradient estimate
g_{0}
be given by

g_{0}=\frac{1}{B_{\textnormal{init}}}\sum_{j=1}^{B_{\textnormal{init}}-1}\nabla f\left(x_{0},\xi_{0,j}\right),

where
B_{\textnormal{init}}=\max\left\{1,\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\varepsilon}\right)^{\frac{p}{p-1}}\right\}
, let the stepsize
\gamma=\min\left\{\sqrt{\frac{\Delta}{L_{1}T}},\sqrt{\frac{\Delta\alpha^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{q}}}}{\sigma_{2}T}},\sqrt[3]{\frac{\Delta\alpha^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}}{L_{2}T}}\right\}
, the momentum parameter
\alpha=\min\left\{1,\alpha_{\textnormal{eff}}\right\}
where

\alpha_{\textnormal{eff}}=\max\left\{\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{\sigma_{1}T}\right)^{\frac{p}{2p-1}},\left(\frac{\Delta\sigma_{2}}{\sigma_{1}^{2}T}\right)^{\frac{pq}{p(2q+1)-2q}},\left(\frac{L_{2}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Delta}{\sigma_{1}^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}T}\right)^{\frac{4p}{7p-6}}\right\}.

Then,
Algorithm

2
guarantees to find an
\varepsilon
–stationary point with the total sample complexity

\mathcal{O}\left(\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\varepsilon}\right)^{\frac{p}{p-1}}+\frac{\Delta}{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{L_{1}+\sigma_{2}}{\varepsilon}+\sqrt{\frac{L_{2}}{\varepsilon}}\right)+\frac{\Delta\sigma_{2}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\varepsilon}\right)^{\frac{p}{q(p-1)}}+\frac{L_{2}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Delta\sigma_{1}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}}}{\varepsilon^{\nicefrac{{7}}{{4}}}}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\varepsilon}\right)^{\frac{p}{4(p-1)}}\right).

Proof.

According to
Lemma

D.4
we have

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|\leq\frac{\Delta}{\gamma T}+\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\hat{e}_{t}\right\|+\frac{\gamma L_{1}}{2},

and using
Lemma

E.1
this yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{t}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}(1-\alpha)^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]+2\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}+2\gamma\sigma_{2}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}+4\gamma^{2}L_{2}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]}{\alpha T}+2\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}+2\gamma\sigma_{2}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}+4\gamma^{2}L_{2}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}},

where in the last term we drop the
\min\left\{\cdots\right\}
to only keep the
\gamma^{2}L_{2}
term. Hence we obtain the bound

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|\right]\leq\frac{\Delta}{\gamma T}+\frac{2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]}{\alpha T}+4\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}+4\gamma\sigma_{2}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}+8\gamma^{2}L_{2}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{\gamma L_{1}}{2}.

Now, using
\gamma=\min\left\{\sqrt{\frac{\Delta}{L_{1}T}},\sqrt{\frac{\Delta\alpha^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{q}}}}{\sigma_{2}T}},\sqrt[3]{\frac{\Delta\alpha^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}}{L_{2}T}}\right\}
we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|\right]=

\displaystyle\qquad\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{L_{1}\Delta}{T}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2q}}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta\sigma_{2}}{T}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{6}}\left(\frac{L_{2}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Delta}{T}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}+\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]}{\alpha T}+\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\right).

(97)

Now, by our choice of
g_{0}
we have, as in we did in the previous section

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}\frac{2\sigma_{1}}{B_{\textnormal{init}}^{\frac{p-1}{p}}},

(98)

and since
B_{\textnormal{init}}=\max\left\{1,\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\varepsilon}\right)^{\frac{p}{p-1}}\right\}
we have
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]\leq\sigma_{1}\times\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{\sigma_{1}}\right)=\varepsilon
. This gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\eqref{36a6b109-a2ff-445f-937c-7040717cb89d-2}}}}{=}\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{L_{1}\Delta}{T}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2q}}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta\sigma_{2}}{T}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{6}}\left(\frac{L_{2}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Delta}{T}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}+\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\right]}{\alpha T}+\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\eqref{c83db8de-7f7d-4c84-82b2-a4e56a709c5f}}}}{=}\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{L_{1}\Delta}{T}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2q}}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta\sigma_{2}}{T}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{6}}\left(\frac{L_{2}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Delta}{T}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}+\frac{\varepsilon}{\alpha T}+\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathcal{O}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{L_{1}\Delta}{T}}+\left[\sqrt{\frac{\Delta\sigma_{2}}{T}}+\alpha_{\textnormal{eff}}^{-\frac{1}{2q}}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta\sigma_{2}}{T}}\right]+\frac{\varepsilon}{T}\alpha_{\textnormal{eff}}^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sigma_{1}\alpha_{\textnormal{eff}}^{\frac{p-1}{p}}+\left[\left(\frac{L_{2}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Delta}{T}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}+\alpha_{\textnormal{eff}}^{-\frac{1}{6}}\left(\frac{L_{2}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Delta}{T}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\scriptscriptstyle\text{(a)}}{=}\mathcal{O}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{L_{1}\Delta}{T}}+\sqrt{\frac{\Delta\sigma_{2}}{T}}+\left(\frac{L_{2}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Delta}{T}\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}+\sigma_{1}\left(\frac{\Delta\sigma_{2}}{\sigma_{1}^{2}T}\right)^{\frac{q(p-1)}{p(2q+1)-2q}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sigma_{1}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{\sigma_{1}T}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p-1}}+\sigma_{1}\left(\frac{L_{2}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Delta}{\sigma_{1}^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}T}\right)^{\frac{4(p-1)}{7p-6}}\Bigg),

(99)

where in (
) we use the choice of
\alpha_{\textnormal{eff}}
from (
E.2
), especially, we have

\sigma_{1}\alpha_{\textnormal{eff}}^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\leq\sigma_{1}\left(\frac{\Delta\sigma_{2}}{\sigma_{1}^{2}T}\right)^{\frac{q(p-1)}{p(2q+1)-2q}}+\sigma_{1}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{\sigma_{1}T}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p-1}}+\sigma_{1}\left(\frac{L_{2}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Delta}{\sigma_{1}^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}T}\right)^{\frac{4(p-1)}{7p-6}}.

Finally, from the bound (
99
) we deduce that the sample complexity of
Algorithm

2
is exactly

\mathcal{O}\left(\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\varepsilon}\right)^{\frac{p}{p-1}}+\frac{\Delta}{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{L_{1}+\sigma_{2}}{\varepsilon}+\sqrt{\frac{L_{2}}{\varepsilon}}\right)+\frac{\Delta\sigma_{2}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\varepsilon}\right)^{\frac{p}{q(p-1)}}+\frac{L_{2}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Delta\sigma_{1}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}}}{\varepsilon^{\nicefrac{{7}}{{4}}}}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\varepsilon}\right)^{\frac{p}{4(p-1)}}\right),

as claimed.
∎

Remark E.1
.

In particular, when
\sigma_{2}=0
we observe that the sample complexity (
E.2
) does not depend on the exponent
q
anymore, as in the lower bound we derived in
Theorem

3.3
.

Appendix F
Missing Proofs in Section
6

F.1
Preliminary Lemmas

Now we start the high-probability convergence analysis of
Algorithm

3
. The proof heavily follows the one of
65
.

F.1.1
Some Descent Lemma

Lemma F.1

(A Descent Lemma (for
Algorithm

1
))
.

Under
2.1
and
2.6
, for any choice of stepsize
\gamma>0
and any choice of momentum parameter
\alpha\in\mathopen{(}0\mathclose{},1\mathclose{]}
,
Algorithm

3
generates iterates
\{x_{t}\}_{t\in\left\{0,\ldots,T\right\}}
which satisfy almost surely (a.s.) the inequality

\displaystyle\gamma\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|+\Delta_{T}\leq\Delta_{0}

\displaystyle+2\gamma\alpha\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}\right\|+2\gamma(1-\alpha)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{2\gamma\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|}{\alpha}+\frac{\gamma^{2}\bar{L}T}{2},

where for any
j\in\left\{1,\ldots,T-1\right\}
, the vectors
\theta_{j}
and
\omega_{j}
are defined as

\displaystyle\theta_{j}:\!

\displaystyle={\texttt{clip}}\left(\nabla f(x_{j},\xi_{j}),\lambda_{2}\right)-\nabla F(x_{j}),

(101)

\displaystyle\omega_{j}:\!

\displaystyle={\texttt{clip}}\left(\nabla f(x_{j},\xi_{j})-\nabla f(x_{j-1},\xi_{j}),\lambda_{1}\right)-\left(\nabla F(x_{j})-\nabla F(x_{j-1})\right),

(102)

and for any
t\in\left\{0,\ldots,T-1\right\}
we let
\Delta_{t}:=F(x_{t})-F^{\inf}
.

Proof.

First of all, let us observe that
Lemma

D.1
still holds for
Algorithm

3
(and can be proved analogously since
Algorithms

1
and
3
have the same gradient update rule). Moreover, as the momentum term does not play any role in the proof of
Lemma

D.1
, the iterates
\{x_{t}\}_{t\in\left\{0,\ldots,T\right\}}
of
Algorithm

3
satisfy almost surely (a.s.)

\gamma\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|+\Delta_{T}\leq\Delta_{0}+2\gamma\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\hat{e}_{t}\right\|+\frac{\gamma^{2}\bar{L}T}{2}.

Next, we bound
\left\|\hat{e}_{t}\right\|
in an analogous way as we did in
Lemma

D.5
. According to the update rule for the momentum term in line
10
of
Algorithm

3
, we have

\displaystyle\hat{e}_{t}

\displaystyle=g_{t}-\nabla F(x_{t})

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha)\left(g_{t-1}+{\texttt{clip}}(\nabla f\left(x_{t},\xi_{t}\right)-\nabla f\left(x_{t-1},\xi_{t}\right),\lambda_{1})\right)+\alpha\,{\texttt{clip}}(\nabla f\left(x_{t},\xi_{t}\right),\lambda_{2})-\nabla F(x_{t})

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha)\left(g_{t-1}-\nabla F(x_{t-1})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+(1-\alpha)\left({\texttt{clip}}(\nabla f\left(x_{t},\xi_{t}\right)-\nabla f\left(x_{t-1},\xi_{t}\right),\lambda_{1})-\left[\nabla F(x_{t})-\nabla F(x_{t-1})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\alpha\left({\texttt{clip}}(\nabla f\left(x_{t},\xi_{t}\right),\lambda_{2})-\nabla F(x_{t})\right)

\displaystyle{=}(1-\alpha)\hat{e}_{t-1}+\alpha\theta_{t}+(1-\alpha)\omega_{t}

\displaystyle{=}(1-\alpha)^{t}\hat{e}_{0}+\alpha\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}+(1-\alpha)\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j},

(104)

where in (
) we unroll the recursion. Then, taking the norm and applying the triangle inequality in this series of equalities, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{e}_{t}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq(1-\alpha)^{t}\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|+\alpha\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}\right\|+(1-\alpha)\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}\right\|,

(105)

and plugging (
105
) in (
F.1.1
) leads to

\displaystyle\gamma\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|+\Delta_{T}

\displaystyle\leq\Delta_{0}+2\gamma\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\hat{e}_{t}\right\|+\frac{\gamma^{2}\bar{L}T}{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Delta_{0}\begin{aligned} &+2\gamma\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left((1-\alpha)^{t}\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|+\alpha\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}\right\|+(1-\alpha)\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}\right\|\right)\\
&+\frac{\gamma^{2}\bar{L}T}{2}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle{\leq}\Delta_{0}\begin{aligned} &+\frac{2\gamma\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|}{\alpha}+\frac{\gamma^{2}\bar{L}T}{2}\\
&+2\gamma\alpha\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}\right\|+2\gamma(1-\alpha)\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}\right\|,\end{aligned}

where in (
) we use the inequality

2\gamma\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}(1-\alpha)^{t}\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\leq 2\gamma\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|\sum_{t\geq 0}(1-\alpha)^{t}=\frac{2\gamma\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|}{\alpha}.

This proves the desired result.
∎

Remark F.1
.

In particular, if we assume
g_{0}=0
in
Algorithm

3
then
x_{0}=x_{1}
and
\Delta_{0}=\Delta_{1}
. Moreover, we have

\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|=\left\|g_{0}-\nabla F(x_{0})\right\|=\left\|\nabla F(x_{1})\right\|\leq\sqrt{2\bar{L}\Delta_{1}},

as by
2.6
we know that
F
has
\bar{L}
–Lipschitz continuous gradients.

Corollary F.1

(Another Descent Lemma)
.

Under
2.1
and
2.2
, for any choice of stepsize
\gamma>0
and any choice of momentum parameter
\alpha\in\mathopen{(}0\mathclose{},1\mathclose{]}
,
Algorithm

3
generates iterates
\{x_{t}\}_{t\in\left\{0,\ldots,T\right\}}
which satisfy almost surely (a.s.) the inequality

\displaystyle\gamma\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|+\Delta_{T}\leq\Delta_{0}

\displaystyle+2\gamma\alpha\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}\right\|+2\gamma(1-\alpha)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{2\gamma\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|}{\alpha}+\frac{\gamma^{2}L_{1}T}{2},

where for any
j\in\left\{1,\ldots,T-1\right\}
, the vectors
\theta_{j}
and
\omega_{j}
are defined in (
101
) and (
102
) and for any
t\in\left\{0,\ldots,T-1\right\}
we let
\Delta_{t}:=F(x_{t})-F^{\inf}
.

Proof.

The proof follows the exact same steps as in the previous lemma (
Lemma

F.1
), with the exception that the function
F
has
L_{1}
–Lipschitz continuous gradients (instead of
\bar{L}
).
∎

Lemma F.2

(A Descent Lemma (for
Algorithm

2
))
.

Under
2.1
and
2.2
, for any choice of stepsize
\gamma>0
and any choice of momentum parameter
\alpha\in\mathopen{(}0\mathclose{},1\mathclose{]}
,
Algorithm

3
generates iterates
\{x_{t}\}_{t\in\left\{0,\ldots,T\right\}}
which satisfy almost surely (a.s.) the inequality

\displaystyle\gamma\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|+\Delta_{T}\leq\Delta_{0}

\displaystyle+2\gamma\alpha\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}\right\|+2\gamma(1-\alpha)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{2\gamma\left\|\hat{e}_{0}\right\|}{\alpha}+\frac{\gamma^{2}L_{1}T}{2},

(106)

where for any
j\in\left\{1,\ldots,T-1\right\}
, the vectors
\theta_{j}
and
\omega_{j}
are defined as

\displaystyle\theta_{j}:\!

\displaystyle={\texttt{clip}}\left(\nabla f(x_{j},\xi_{j}),\lambda_{2}\right)-\nabla F(x_{j}),

(107)

\displaystyle\omega_{j}:\!

\displaystyle={\texttt{clip}}\left(\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{j},\hat{\xi}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1}),\lambda_{1}\right)-\left(\nabla F(x_{j})-\nabla F(x_{j-1})\right),

(108)

and for any
t\in\left\{0,\ldots,T-1\right\}
we let
\Delta_{t}:=F(x_{t})-F^{\inf}
.

Proof.

The proof is very similar to the proof of
Lemma

F.1
with the exception that the here function
F
has
L_{1}
–Lipschitz continuous gradients (instead of
\bar{L}
) and the computations (
104
) varies slightly due to the use of the Hessian term
\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{j},\hat{\xi}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1})
instead of the difference
\nabla f(x_{j},\xi_{j})-\nabla f(x_{j-1},\xi_{j-1})
. We thus have,

\displaystyle\hat{e}_{t}

\displaystyle=g_{t}-\nabla F(x_{t})

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha)\left(g_{t-1}+{\texttt{clip}}(\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{t},\hat{\xi}_{t})(x_{t}-x_{t-1}),\lambda_{1})\right)+\alpha\,{\texttt{clip}}(\nabla f\left(x_{t},\xi_{t}\right),\lambda_{2})-\nabla F(x_{t})

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha)\left(g_{t-1}-\nabla F(x_{t-1})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+(1-\alpha)\left({\texttt{clip}}(\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{t},\hat{\xi}_{t})(x_{t}-x_{t-1}),\lambda_{1})-\left[\nabla F(x_{t})-\nabla F(x_{t-1})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\alpha\left({\texttt{clip}}(\nabla f\left(x_{t},\xi_{t}\right),\lambda_{2})-\nabla F(x_{t})\right)

\displaystyle{=}(1-\alpha)\hat{e}_{t-1}+\alpha\theta_{t}+(1-\alpha)\omega_{t}

\displaystyle{=}(1-\alpha)^{t}\hat{e}_{0}+\alpha\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}+(1-\alpha)\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j},

where in (
) we unroll the recursion. The next steps are exactly as in the proof of
Lemma

F.1
and the claimed result (
106
) follows.
∎

F.1.2
High-Probability Analysis

From the previous
Lemma

F.1
, we need to bound the two new terms:

\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}\right\|\,\,\text{ and }\,\,\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}\right\|,

where
t\in\left\{0,\ldots,T-1\right\}
. To do so, we use the same strategy as in previous works
(
28
;
64
;
48
;
65
)
and we introduce the biased and unbiased parts of
\theta_{j}
and
\omega_{j}
, that is, for any
j\in[T-1]
we let
\theta_{j}=\theta_{j}^{b}+\theta_{j}^{u}
with

\displaystyle\theta_{j}^{b}:\!

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{t}}\left[{\texttt{clip}}\left(\nabla f(x_{t},\xi_{t}),\lambda_{1}\right)\right]-\nabla F(x_{t}),

(109)

\displaystyle\theta_{j}^{u}:\!

\displaystyle={\texttt{clip}}\left(\nabla f(x_{t},\xi_{t}),\lambda_{2}\right)-{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{t}}\left[{\texttt{clip}}\left(\nabla f(x_{t},\xi_{t}),\lambda_{2}\right)\right],

(110)

and
\omega_{j}=\omega_{j}^{b}+\omega_{j}^{u}
where

\displaystyle\omega_{j}^{b}

\displaystyle:={\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{t}}\left[{\texttt{clip}}\left(\nabla f(x_{j},\xi_{j})-\nabla f(x_{j-1},\xi_{j}),\lambda_{1}\right)\right]-\left(\nabla F(x_{j})-\nabla F(x_{j-1})\right),

(111)

\displaystyle\omega_{j}^{u}

\displaystyle:={\texttt{clip}}\left(\nabla f(x_{j},\xi_{j})-\nabla f(x_{j-1},\xi_{j}),\lambda_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{t}}\left[{\texttt{clip}}\left(\nabla f(x_{j},\xi_{j})-\nabla f(x_{j-1},\xi_{j}),\lambda_{1}\right)\right]

(112)

for
Lemma

F.1
and
Corollary

F.1
. For
Lemma

F.2
(hessian clipping) we let
\omega_{j}=\omega_{j}^{r}+\omega_{j}^{b}+\omega_{j}^{u}
where

\displaystyle\omega_{j}^{r}:\!

\displaystyle=\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1})-\left(\nabla F(x_{j})-\nabla F(x_{j-1})\right),

(113)

\displaystyle\omega_{j}^{b}:\!

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{\hat{\xi}_{t}}\left[{\texttt{clip}}\left(\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{j},\hat{\xi}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1}),\lambda_{1}\right)\right]-\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1}),

(114)

\displaystyle\omega_{j}^{u}:\!

\displaystyle={\texttt{clip}}\left(\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{j},\hat{\xi}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1}),\lambda_{1}\right)-{\mathbb{E}}_{\hat{\xi}_{t}}\left[{\texttt{clip}}\left(\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{j},\hat{\xi}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1}),\lambda_{1}\right)\right]

(115)

Lemma F.3
.

Under
2.3
, for any
\delta^{\prime}\in\mathopen{(}0\mathclose{},\frac{1}{2}\mathclose{]}
and any
t\in\left\{0,\ldots,T-1\right\}
, if the clipping threshold satisfies

\lambda_{2}\geq\max\left\{2\left\|\nabla F(x_{j})\right\|,\sigma_{1}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{p}}\right\},

for all
j\in[t]
then, with probability at least
1-2\delta^{\prime}
, we have

\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}\right\|\leq 22\lambda_{2}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}.

Proof.

First of all, using (
109
) and (
110
) it follows

\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}\right\|\leq\underbrace{\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{b}\right\|}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 4$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 4$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 4$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 4$}}}}}}+\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|,

and now, we need to bound both terms above. For the second term, we use
Lemma

G.2
with exponent
2
to obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|

\displaystyle{\leq}\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}V_{j}^{t}\right\rvert+\sqrt{\max_{j\in[t]}\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{t}\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}V_{j}^{t}\right\rvert+\sqrt{2\sum_{j=1}^{t}\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle{=}\underbrace{\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}V_{j}^{t}\right\rvert}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}}}+\sqrt{2\underbrace{\sum_{j=1}^{t}Y_{j}^{t}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}}}+\underbrace{\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 3$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 3$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 3$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}}}}}},

(116)

where in (
) we define the sequence
V_{1}^{t},\ldots,V_{t}^{t}
as in
Lemma

G.2
, that is,

\displaystyle V_{j}^{t}:=\begin{cases}0,&\text{if $j=0$};\\
\mathop{\mathrm{sign}}\left(\sum\limits_{i=1}^{j-1}V_{i}^{t}\right)\frac{\left\langle\sum\limits_{i=1}^{j-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-i}\theta_{i}^{u},(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\rangle}{\left\|\sum\limits_{i=1}^{j-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-i}\theta_{i}^{u}\right\|},&\text{if $j\neq 0$ and $\sum\limits_{i=1}^{j-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-i}\theta_{i}^{u}\neq 0$};\\
0,&\text{if $j\neq 0$ and $\sum\limits_{i=1}^{j-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-i}\theta_{i}^{u}=0$}.\end{cases}

while in (
), for any
j\in[t]
we let

Y_{j}^{t}:=\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right].

We now bound all terms
1
,
2
,
3
and
4
.

Bounding
1
.

The sequence
V_{1}^{t},\ldots,V_{t}^{t}
is a martingale difference sequence since, by definition of
\theta_{j}^{u}
and
V_{j}^{t}
, for all
j\in[t]
we have
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.V_{j}^{t}\,\right|\,V_{j-1}^{t},\ldots,V_{1}^{t}\right]=0
since
{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\theta_{j}^{u}\right]=0
. Moreover, by
Lemma

G.2
we also have

\left\lvert V_{j}^{t}\right\rvert\leq\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|\leq\left\|\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Lem.}\!~\text{\ref{appdx-technical-lem:sadiev-generalization}}}}{\leq}2\lambda_{2},

and let
c_{2}:=2\lambda_{2}
be the upper bound on the random variables
V_{1}^{t},\ldots,V_{t}^{t}
. Additionally, if we denote by
\sigma_{j}^{2}:={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left(V_{j}^{t}\right)^{2}\,\right|\,V_{j-1}^{t},\ldots,V_{1}^{t}\right]
then by
Lemma

G.3
for any real number
b_{2}>0
and any
G_{2}>0
, as
0<\delta^{\prime}\leq\frac{1}{2}
then
\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}\geq 1
and we have

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}V_{j}^{t}\right\rvert>b_{2}\,\text{ and }\,\sum_{j=1}^{t}\sigma_{j}^{2}\leq G_{2}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{b_{2}^{2}}{2G_{2}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}+\frac{2b_{2}c_{2}}{3}}\right)=\delta^{\prime},

where the last equality holds provided
b_{2}:=\left(\frac{c_{2}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{c_{2}^{2}}{9}+2G_{2}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}>0
. We now need to define the constant
G_{2}
. To do so, we need to bound the sum
\sigma_{1}^{2}+\cdots+\sigma_{t}^{2}
, this gives

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{t}\sigma_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left(V_{j}^{t}\right)^{2}\,\right|\,V_{j-1}^{t},\ldots,V_{1}^{t}\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{2(t-j)}\,{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}18\lambda_{2}^{2-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{2(t-j)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{18\lambda_{2}^{2-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{1-(1-\alpha)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{18\lambda_{2}^{2-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{\alpha},

(119)

where in the application of
Lemma

G.4
we require
\lambda_{2}\geq 2\max_{j\in[t]}\left\|\nabla F(x_{j})\right\|
. Setting
G_{2}:=\frac{18\lambda_{2}^{2-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{\alpha}>0
gives the desired bound (
F.1.2
).

Bounding
2
.

As in the previous paragraph, the sequence
Y_{1}^{t},\ldots,Y_{t}^{t}
is also a martingale difference sequence as the definition of
(Y_{j}^{t})_{j\in[t]}
implies
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.Y_{j}^{t}\,\right|\,Y_{j-1}^{t}\ldots,Y_{1}^{t}\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[Y_{j}^{t}\right]=0
for all
j\in[t]
. Moreover, according to
Lemma

G.4
, we also have, as we did in (
F.1.2
), for any
j\in[t]

\left\lvert Y_{j}^{t}\right\rvert\leq\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq 4\lambda_{2}^{2}+4\lambda_{2}^{2}=8\lambda_{2}^{2}.

Hence we define
\widetilde{c}_{2}:=8\lambda_{2}^{2}
. Now, denoting the conditional variance of
Y_{j}^{t}
as

\widetilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}:={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left(Y_{j}^{t}\right)^{2}\,\right|\,Y_{j-1}^{t},\ldots,Y_{1}^{t}\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left(Y_{j}^{t}\right)^{2}\right]

we have the bound

\displaystyle\widetilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle{\leq}8\lambda_{2}^{2}\,{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\lvert Y_{j}^{t}\right\rvert\right]\leq 16\lambda_{2}^{2}\,{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right],

(121)

for all
j\in[t]
. Hence,

\sum_{j=1}^{t}\widetilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\eqref{a26983bb-60b7-49ab-ac0e-a1d026f1c51e}}}}{\leq}16\lambda_{2}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\eqref{74b4e2bb-4754-4dd3-ae90-37ef40362f93}}}}{\leq}16\lambda_{2}^{2}\cdot\frac{18\lambda_{2}^{2-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{\alpha}=\frac{288\lambda_{2}^{4-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{\alpha},

(where we require
\lambda_{2}\geq 2\max_{j\in[t]}\left\|\nabla F(x_{j})\right\|
). Next, if we define
\widetilde{G}_{2}:=\frac{288\lambda_{2}^{4-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{\alpha}
then, applying
Lemma

G.3
we obtain for any real number
\widetilde{b}_{2}

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}Y_{j}^{t}\right\rvert>\widetilde{b}_{2}\,\text{ and }\,\sum_{j=1}^{t}\widetilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}\leq\widetilde{G}_{2}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{\widetilde{b}_{2}^{2}}{2\widetilde{G}_{2}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}+\frac{2\widetilde{b}_{2}\widetilde{c}_{2}}{3}}\right)=\delta^{\prime},

where the last identity holds if we set
\widetilde{b}_{2}:=\left(\frac{\widetilde{c}_{2}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{\widetilde{c}_{2}^{2}}{9}+2\widetilde{G}_{2}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}
. This establishes the desired bound.

Bounding
3
.

As we already did in the two last paragraphs, we have

\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 3$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 3$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 3$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}}}}:=\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\eqref{74b4e2bb-4754-4dd3-ae90-37ef40362f93}}}}{\leq}\frac{18\lambda_{2}^{2-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{\alpha},

as desired.

Bounding
4
.

If we assume that
\lambda_{2}\geq 2\left\|\nabla F(x_{j})\right\|
for all
j\in[t]
then, with probability one we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 4$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 4$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 4$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 4$}}}}}:\!

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{b}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\left\|\theta_{j}^{b}\right\|

\displaystyle{\leq}4\lambda_{2}^{1-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\lambda_{2}^{1-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{\alpha},

where in (
) we use
Lemma

G.4
, more precisely, for any
j\in[t]
,

\left\|\theta_{j}^{b}\right\|\leq 2^{p}\lambda_{2}^{1-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}\leq 4\lambda_{2}^{1-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}.

Summing up all bounds
1
,
2
,
3
and
4
.

To sum up, we introduce the event
E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}},t}
as follows

E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}},t}:=\left\{\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}V_{j}^{t}\right\rvert\leq b_{2}\,\text{ or }\,\sum_{j=1}^{t}\sigma_{j}^{2}>G_{2}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}\right\},

where we defined
c_{2}:=2\lambda_{2}
,
G_{2}:=\frac{18\lambda_{2}^{2-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{\alpha}
and
b_{2}:=\left(\frac{c_{2}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{c_{2}^{2}}{9}+2G_{2}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}
and we can bound
b_{2}
as

\displaystyle b_{2}:=\left(\frac{c_{2}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{c_{2}^{2}}{9}+2G_{2}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2c_{2}}{3}+\sqrt{2G_{2}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{4\lambda_{2}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{36\lambda_{2}^{2-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{\alpha}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{2}\left(\frac{4}{3}+6\sqrt{\frac{1}{\alpha}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\lambda_{2}}\right)^{p}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle{\leq}\frac{22\lambda_{2}}{3}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}},

(123)

where (
) holds provided
\lambda_{2}\geq\sigma_{1}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{p}}
. On the other hand, we also define the event
E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}},t}
as follows

E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}},t}:=\left\{\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}Y_{j}^{t}\right\rvert\leq\widetilde{b}_{2}\,\text{ or }\,\sum_{j=1}^{t}\widetilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}>\widetilde{G}_{2}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}\right\},

where we defined
\widetilde{c}_{2}:=8\lambda_{2}^{2}
,
\widetilde{G}_{2}:=\frac{288\lambda_{2}^{4-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{\alpha}
and
\widetilde{b}_{2}:=\left(\frac{\widetilde{c}_{2}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{\widetilde{c}_{2}^{2}}{9}+2\widetilde{G}_{2}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}
and we can bound
\widetilde{b}_{2}
as

\displaystyle\widetilde{b}_{2}:=\left(\frac{\widetilde{c}_{2}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{\widetilde{c}_{2}^{2}}{9}+2\widetilde{G}_{2}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2\widetilde{c}_{2}}{3}+\sqrt{2\widetilde{G}_{2}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{16\lambda_{2}^{2}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{576\lambda_{2}^{4-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{\alpha}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{2}^{2}\left(\frac{16}{3}+24\sqrt{\frac{1}{\alpha}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\lambda_{2}}\right)^{p}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{88\lambda_{2}^{2}}{3}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}},

(124)

where the last inequality holds given
\lambda_{2}\geq\sigma_{1}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{p}}
.

Next, given
\lambda_{2}\geq\max\left\{2\left\|\nabla F(x_{j})\right\|,\sigma_{1}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{p}}\right\}
for all
j\in[t]
we proved in (
F.1.2
) and (
F.1.2
) that
\mathbb{P}\left(E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}},t}\right)\geq 1-\delta^{\prime}
and
\mathbb{P}\left(E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}},t}\right)\geq 1-\delta^{\prime}
hence, by the union bound inequality we have

\mathbb{P}\left(E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}},t}\cap E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}},t}\right)\geq 1-2\delta^{\prime},

and on the event
E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}},t}\cap E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}},t}
we obtain the inequality

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{b}\right\|+\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}^{u}\right\|

\displaystyle{\leq}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}}+\sqrt{2\cdot\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}}+2\cdot\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 3$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 3$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 3$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}}}}}+\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 4$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 4$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 4$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 4$}}}}}

\displaystyle{\leq}\frac{22\lambda_{2}}{3}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}+\sqrt{2\cdot\frac{88\lambda_{2}^{2}}{3}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}+2\cdot\frac{18\lambda_{2}^{2-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{\alpha}}+\frac{4\lambda_{2}^{1-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{\alpha}

\displaystyle{\leq}\lambda_{2}\left(\frac{22}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{176}{3}+\frac{36}{\alpha}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\lambda_{2}}\right)^{p}}+\frac{4}{\alpha}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\lambda_{2}}\right)^{p}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle{\leq}\lambda_{2}\left(\frac{22}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{176}{3}+36}+4\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq 22\lambda_{2}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}},

where in (
) we use the fact that
\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}\geq 1
and in (
) we use
\lambda_{2}\geq\sigma_{1}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{p}}
. This proves the lemma.
∎

Lemma F.4
.

Under
2.6
, for any
\delta^{\prime\prime}\in\mathopen{(}0\mathclose{},\frac{1}{2}\mathclose{]}
and any
t\in\left\{0,\ldots,T-1\right\}
, if the clipping threshold satisfies

\lambda_{1}\geq\max\left\{2\gamma\bar{L},\gamma\bar{L}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}\right\},

for all
j\in[t]
then, with probability at least
1-2\delta^{\prime\prime}
, we have

\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}\right\|\leq 46\lambda_{1}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}.

Proof.

First, using (
111
) and (
112
) we have

\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}\right\|\leq\underbrace{\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{b}\right\|}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 8$}}}}}}+\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|,

and, as before, we need to bound both terms above. For the second term, we use
Lemma

G.2
with exponent
2
to obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|

\displaystyle{\leq}\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}W_{j}^{t}\right\rvert+\sqrt{\max_{j\in[t]}\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{t}\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}W_{j}^{t}\right\rvert+\sqrt{2\sum_{j=1}^{t}\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle{=}\underbrace{\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}W_{j}^{t}\right\rvert}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 5$}}}}}}+\sqrt{2\underbrace{\sum_{j=1}^{t}Z_{j}^{t}}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 6$}}}}}}+\underbrace{\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 7$}}}}}}},

(126)

where in (
) we define the sequence
V_{1}^{t},\ldots,V_{t}^{t}
as in
Lemma

G.2
, that is,

\displaystyle W_{j}^{t}:=\begin{cases}0,&\text{if $j=0$};\\
\mathop{\mathrm{sign}}\left(\sum\limits_{i=1}^{j-1}W_{i}^{t}\right)\frac{\left\langle\sum\limits_{i=1}^{j-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-i}\omega_{i}^{u},(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\rangle}{\left\|\sum\limits_{i=1}^{j-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-i}\omega_{i}^{u}\right\|},&\text{if $j\neq 0$ and $\sum\limits_{i=1}^{j-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-i}\omega_{i}^{u}\neq 0$};\\
0,&\text{if $j\neq 0$ and $\sum\limits_{i=1}^{j-1}(1-\alpha)^{t-i}\omega_{i}^{u}=0$}.\end{cases}

while in (
), for any
j\in[t]
we let

Z_{j}^{t}:=\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right].

We now bound all terms
5
,
6
,
7
and
8
.

Bounding
5
.

The sequence
W_{1}^{t},\ldots,W_{t}^{t}
is a martingale difference sequence since, by definition of
\omega_{j}^{u}
and
W_{j}^{t}
, for all
j\in[t]
we have
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.W_{j}^{t}\,\right|\,W_{j-1}^{t},\ldots,W_{1}^{t}\right]=0
since
{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\omega_{j}^{u}\right]=0
. Moreover, by
Lemma

G.2
we also have

\left\lvert W_{j}^{t}\right\rvert\leq\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|\leq\left\|\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Lem.}\!~\text{\ref{appdx-technical-lem:sadiev-generalization}}}}{\leq}2\lambda_{1},

and let
c_{1}:=2\lambda_{1}
be the upper bound on the random variables
W_{1}^{t},\ldots,W_{t}^{t}
. Additionally, if we denote by
\sigma_{j}^{2}:={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left(W_{j}^{t}\right)^{2}\,\right|\,W_{j-1}^{t},\ldots,W_{1}^{t}\right]
then by
Lemma

G.3
for any real number
b_{1}>0
and any
G_{1}>0
, as
0<\delta^{\prime\prime}\leq\frac{1}{2}
then
\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}\geq 1
and we have

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}W_{j}^{t}\right\rvert>b_{1}\,\text{ and }\,\sum_{j=1}^{t}\sigma_{j}^{2}\leq G_{1}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{b_{1}^{2}}{2G_{1}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2b_{1}c_{1}}{3}}\right)=\delta^{\prime\prime},

where the last equality holds provided
b_{1}:=\left(\frac{c_{1}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{c_{1}^{2}}{9}+2G_{1}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}>0
. We now need to define the constant
G_{1}
. To do so, we need to bound the sum
\sigma_{1}^{2}+\cdots+\sigma_{t}^{2}
, this gives

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{t}\sigma_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left(W_{j}^{t}\right)^{2}\,\right|\,W_{j-1}^{t},\ldots,W_{1}^{t}\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{2(t-j)}\,{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}72\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{2(t-j)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{72\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}}{1-(1-\alpha)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{72\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}}{\alpha},

(129)

where in the application of
Lemma

G.4
we use the bound we proved earlier in
Lemma

D.5
(see more precisely at (
71
)), that is,

{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|\left[\nabla F(x_{j})-\nabla F(x_{j-1})\right]-\left[\nabla f(x_{j},\xi_{j})-\nabla f(x_{j-1},\xi_{j})\right]\right\|^{q}\right]\leq 2^{q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}.

Moreover, applying
Lemma

G.4
requires to take
\lambda_{1}\geq 2\max_{j\in[t]}\left\|\nabla F(x_{j})-\nabla F(x_{j-1})\right\|
and, since

\left\|\nabla F(x_{j})-\nabla F(x_{j-1})\right\|\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Ass.}\!~\text{\ref{ass:mean-squared-smoothness}}}}{\leq}\bar{L}\left\|x_{j}-x_{j-1}\right\|=\gamma\bar{L},

then it is enough to have
\lambda_{1}\geq 2\gamma\bar{L}
. Setting
G_{1}:=\frac{72\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}}{\alpha}>0
gives the desired bound (
F.1.2
).

Bounding
6
.

As in the previous paragraph, the sequence
Z_{1}^{t},\ldots,Z_{t}^{t}
is also a martingale difference sequence as the definition of
(Z_{j}^{t})_{j\in[t]}
implies
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.Z_{j}^{t}\,\right|\,Z_{j-1}^{t}\ldots,Z_{1}^{t}\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[Z_{j}^{t}\right]=0
for all
j\in[t]
. Moreover, according to
Lemma

G.4
, we also have, as we did in (
F.1.2
), for any
j\in[t]

\left\lvert Z_{j}^{t}\right\rvert\leq\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq 4\lambda_{1}^{2}+4\lambda_{1}^{2}=8\lambda_{1}^{2}.

Hence we define
\widetilde{c}_{1}:=8\lambda_{1}^{2}
. Now, denoting the conditional variance of
Z_{j}^{t}
as

\widetilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}:={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left(Z_{j}^{t}\right)^{2}\,\right|\,Z_{j-1}^{t},\ldots,Z_{1}^{t}\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left(Z_{j}^{t}\right)^{2}\right]

we have the bound

\displaystyle\widetilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle{\leq}8\lambda_{1}^{2}\,{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\lvert Z_{j}^{t}\right\rvert\right]\leq 16\lambda_{1}^{2}\,{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right],

(131)

for all
j\in[t]
. Hence,

\sum_{j=1}^{t}\widetilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\eqref{a26983bb-60b7-49ab-ac0e-a1d026f1c51e-2}}}}{\leq}16\lambda_{1}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\eqref{74b4e2bb-4754-4dd3-ae90-37ef40362f93-2}}}}{\leq}16\lambda_{1}^{2}\cdot\frac{72\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}}{\alpha}=\frac{1152\lambda_{1}^{4-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}}{\alpha},

(where we require
\lambda_{1}\geq 2\gamma\bar{L}
). Next, if we define
\widetilde{G}_{1}:=\frac{1152\lambda_{1}^{4-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}}{\alpha}
then, applying
Lemma

G.3
we obtain for any real number
\widetilde{b}_{2}

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}Z_{j}^{t}\right\rvert>\widetilde{b}_{1}\,\text{ and }\,\sum_{j=1}^{t}\widetilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}\leq\widetilde{G}_{1}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{\widetilde{b}_{1}^{2}}{2\widetilde{G}_{1}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2\widetilde{b}_{1}\widetilde{c}_{1}}{3}}\right)=\delta^{\prime\prime},

where the last identity holds if we set
\widetilde{b}_{1}:=\left(\frac{\widetilde{c}_{1}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{\widetilde{c}_{1}^{2}}{9}+2\widetilde{G}_{1}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}
. This establishes the desired bound.

Bounding
7
.

As we already did in the two last paragraphs, we have

\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 7$}}}}}:=\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\eqref{74b4e2bb-4754-4dd3-ae90-37ef40362f93-2}}}}{\leq}\frac{72\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}}{\alpha},

as desired.

Bounding
8
.

If we assume that
\lambda_{1}\geq 2\gamma\bar{L}
then, with probability one we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 8$}}}}}:\!

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{b}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\left\|\omega_{j}^{b}\right\|

\displaystyle{\leq}16\lambda_{1}^{1-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16\lambda_{1}^{1-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}}{\alpha},

where in (
) we use
Lemma

G.4
, more precisely, for any
j\in[t]
,

\left\|\omega_{j}^{b}\right\|\leq 4^{q}\lambda_{1}^{1-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}\leq 16\lambda_{1}^{1-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q},

since
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\left[\nabla F(x_{t})-\nabla F(x_{t-1})\right]-\left[\nabla f(x_{t},\xi_{t})-\nabla f(x_{t-1},\xi_{t})\right]\right\|^{q}\right]\leq 2^{q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}
by
Lemma

D.5
(and more precisely by (
71
)).

Summing up all bounds
5
,
6
,
7
and
8
.

To sum up, we introduce the event
E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 5$}}}}},t}
as follows

E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 5$}}}}},t}:=\left\{\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}W_{j}^{t}\right\rvert\leq b_{1}\,\text{ or }\,\sum_{j=1}^{t}\sigma_{j}^{2}>G_{1}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}\right\},

where we defined
c_{1}:=2\lambda_{1}
,
G_{1}:=\frac{72\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}}{\alpha}
and
b_{1}:=\left(\frac{c_{1}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{c_{1}^{2}}{9}+2G_{1}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}
and we can bound
b_{1}
as

\displaystyle b_{1}:=\left(\frac{c_{1}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{c_{1}^{2}}{9}+2G_{1}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2c_{1}}{3}+\sqrt{2G_{1}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{4\lambda_{1}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{144\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}}{\alpha}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{1}\left(\frac{4}{3}+12\sqrt{\frac{1}{\alpha}\left(\frac{\gamma\bar{L}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)^{q}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle{\leq}\frac{40\lambda_{1}}{3}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}},

(134)

where (
) holds provided
\lambda_{1}\geq\gamma\bar{L}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}
. On the other hand, we also define the event
E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 6$}}}}},t}
as follows

E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 6$}}}}},t}:=\left\{\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}Z_{j}^{t}\right\rvert\leq\widetilde{b}_{1}\,\text{ or }\,\sum_{j=1}^{t}\widetilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}>\widetilde{G}_{1}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}\right\},

,

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{16\lambda_{1}^{2}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{2304\lambda_{1}^{4-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}}{\alpha}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}

(136)

where the last inequality holds given

.

6$}}}}}+2\cdot\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle \displaystyle{\leq}\frac{40\lambda_{1}}{3}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}+\sqrt{2\cdot\frac{160\lambda_{1}^{2}}{3}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}+2\cdot\frac{72\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}}{\alpha}}+\frac{16\lambda_{1}^{1-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}}{\alpha}

\displaystyle{\leq}\lambda_{1}\left(\frac{40}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{320}{3}+\frac{144}{\alpha}\left(\frac{\gamma\bar{L}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)^{q}}+\frac{16}{\alpha}\left(\frac{\gamma\bar{L}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)^{q}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle{\leq}\lambda_{1}\left(\frac{40}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{320}{3}+144}+16\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq 46\lambda_{1}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}},

where in (
) we use the fact that
\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}\geq 1
and in (
) we use
\lambda_{1}\geq\gamma\bar{L}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}
. This proves the lemma.
∎

Lemma F.5
.

Under
2.2
and
2.7
, for any
\delta^{\prime\prime}\in\mathopen{(}0\mathclose{},\frac{1}{2}\mathclose{]}
and any

\lambda_{1}\geq\max\left\{2\gamma L_{1},\gamma\bar{\delta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}\right\},

for all

then, with probability at least
1-2\delta^{\prime\prime}
