8$}}}}}:=\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{b}\right\|.

Bounding
5
.

As in the previous lemma, we choose
c_{1}:=2\lambda_{1}
and
b_{1}:=\left(\frac{c_{1}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{c_{1}^{2}}{9}+2G_{1}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}>0
. Then, to select
G_{1}>0
we need to bound the sum
\sigma_{1}^{2}+\cdots+\sigma_{t}^{2}
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{t}\sigma_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle{\leq}\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{2(t-j)}\,{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}18\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q}\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{2(t-j)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{18\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q}}{\alpha},

(137)

where
\bar{\delta}\geq 0
is the parameter in
2.7
. In the application of
Lemma

G.4
we use
2.7

{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|\left[\nabla F(x_{j})-\nabla F(x_{j-1})\right]-\left[\nabla f(x_{j},\xi_{j})-\nabla f(x_{j-1},\xi_{j})\right]\right\|^{q}\right]\leq\bar{\delta}^{q}\left\|x_{j}-x_{j-1}\right\|^{q}\leq\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q}.

Moreover, applying
Lemma

G.4
requires to take
\lambda_{1}\geq 2\max_{j\in[t]}\left\|\nabla F(x_{j})-\nabla F(x_{j-1})\right\|
and, since

\left\|\nabla F(x_{j})-\nabla F(x_{j-1})\right\|\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Ass.}\!~\text{\ref{ass:L-lipschitz-gradients}}}}{\leq}L_{1}\left\|x_{j}-x_{j-1}\right\|=\gamma L_{1},

then it is enough to have
\lambda_{1}\geq 2\gamma L_{1}
. Setting
G_{1}:=\frac{18\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q}}{\alpha}>0
gives the desired bound.

Bounding
6
.

Similarly to the previous lemma, we set
\widetilde{c}_{1}:=8\lambda_{1}^{2}
and
\widetilde{b}_{1}:=\left(\frac{\widetilde{c}_{1}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{\widetilde{c}_{1}^{2}}{9}+2\widetilde{G}_{1}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}
. For the choice
\widetilde{G}_{1}
, we have the bound

\sum_{j=1}^{t}\widetilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\eqref{a26983bb-60b7-49ab-ac0e-a1d026f1c51e-2}}}}{\leq}16\lambda_{1}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\eqref{74b4e2bb-4754-4dd3-ae90-37ef40362f93-3}}}}{\leq}16\lambda_{1}^{2}\cdot\frac{18\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q}}{\alpha}=\frac{288\lambda_{1}^{4-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q}}{\alpha},

(where we require
\lambda_{1}\geq 2\gamma L_{1}
). Hence, if we let
\widetilde{G}_{1}:=\frac{288\lambda_{1}^{4-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q}}{\alpha}
this establishes the desired bound.

Bounding
7
.

As we already did in the two last paragraphs, we have

\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 7$}}}}}:=\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{\xi_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\eqref{74b4e2bb-4754-4dd3-ae90-37ef40362f93-3}}}}{\leq}\frac{18\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q}}{\alpha},

as desired.

Bounding
8
.

For the last bound, if we assume that
\lambda_{1}\geq 2\gamma L_{1}
then, with probability one we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 8$}}}}}:\!

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{b}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\left\|\omega_{j}^{b}\right\|

\displaystyle{\leq}4\lambda_{1}^{1-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q}\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\lambda_{1}^{1-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q}}{\alpha},

where in (
) we use
Lemma

G.4
, more precisely, for any
j\in[t]
,

\left\|\omega_{j}^{b}\right\|\leq 2^{q}\lambda_{1}^{1-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q}\leq 4\lambda_{1}^{1-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q},

since
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\left[\nabla F(x_{t})-\nabla F(x_{t-1})\right]-\left[\nabla f(x_{t},\xi_{t})-\nabla f(x_{t-1},\xi_{t})\right]\right\|^{q}\right]\leq\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q}
by
2.7
.

Summing up all bounds
5
,

,

and

.

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2c_{1}}{3}+\sqrt{2G_{1}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{4\lambda_{1}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{36\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q}}{\alpha}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}

.

hence, by the union bound inequality we have

and on the event

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}\right\|

\displaystyle{\leq}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 5$}}}}}+\sqrt{2\cdot\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 6$}}}}}+2\cdot\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 7$}}}}}}+\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 8$}}}}}

\displaystyle{\leq}\frac{22\lambda_{1}}{3}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}+\sqrt{2\cdot\frac{176\lambda_{1}^{2}}{3}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}+2\cdot\frac{18\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q}}{\alpha}}+\frac{4\lambda_{1}^{1-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{\delta}^{q}}{\alpha}

\displaystyle{\leq}\lambda_{1}\left(\frac{22}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{176}{3}+\frac{36}{\alpha}\left(\frac{\gamma\bar{\delta}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)^{q}}+\frac{4}{\alpha}\left(\frac{\gamma\bar{\delta}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)^{q}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle{\leq}\lambda_{1}\left(\frac{22}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{176}{3}+36}+4\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq 22\lambda_{1}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}},

where in (
) we use the fact that
\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}\geq 1
and in (
) we use
\lambda_{1}\geq\gamma\bar{\delta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}
. This proves the lemma.
∎

Lemma F.6
.

Under
2.2
,
2.4
and
2.5
, for any
\delta^{\prime\prime}\in\mathopen{(}0\mathclose{},\frac{1}{2}\mathclose{]}
and any
t\in\left\{0,\ldots,T-1\right\}
, if the clipping threshold satisfies

\lambda_{1}\geq\max\left\{2\gamma L_{1},\gamma\sigma_{2}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}\right\},

for all
j\in[t]
then, with probability at least
1-2\delta^{\prime\prime}
, we have

\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}\right\|\leq\frac{2\min\left\{\gamma L_{1},\gamma^{2}L_{2}\right\}}{\alpha}+22\lambda_{1}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}.

Proof.

First, using (
113
), (
114
) and (
115
) we have

\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}\right\|\leq\underbrace{\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{r}\right\|}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 9$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 9$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 9$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 9$}}}}}}+\underbrace{\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{b}\right\|}_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 8$}}}}}}+\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|,

) is identical and we now need to bound all terms

5$}}}}}:=\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}W_{j}^{t}\right\rvert,\quad\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle }\,\,\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 7$}}}}}:=\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{\hat{\xi}_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right],

and
W_{1}^{t},\ldots,W_{t}^{t}
is a martingale difference sequence since, by definition of
\omega_{j}^{u}
and
W_{j}^{t}
, for all
j\in[t]
we have
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.W_{j}^{t}\,\right|\,W_{j-1}^{t},\ldots,W_{1}^{t}\right]=0
since

{\mathbb{E}}_{q_{j},\hat{\xi}_{j}}\left[\omega_{j}^{u}\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{q_{j}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}_{\hat{\xi}_{j}}\left[\left.\omega_{j}^{u}\,\right|\,q_{j}\right]\right]=0.

The same argument applies to the sequence
Z_{1}^{t},\ldots,Z_{t}^{t}
.

Bounding
5
.

As already done before, we choose
c_{1}:=2\lambda_{1}
and
b_{1}:=\left(\frac{c_{1}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{c_{1}^{2}}{9}+2G_{1}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}>0
. Then, to select
G_{1}>0
we need to bound the sum
\sigma_{1}^{2}+\cdots+\sigma_{t}^{2}
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{t}\sigma_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle{\leq}\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{2(t-j)}\,{\mathbb{E}}_{\hat{\xi}_{j}}\left[\left\|\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle{\leq}18\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q}\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{2(t-j)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{18\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q}}{\alpha}.

(141)

In the application of
Lemma

G.4
we use
2.5

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\hat{\xi}_{j}}\left[\left\|\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{j},\hat{\xi}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1})-\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1})\right\|^{q}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\hat{\xi}_{j}}\left[\left\|\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{j},\hat{\xi}_{j})-\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{j})\right\|_{\text{op}}^{q}\cdot\left\|x_{j}-x_{j-1}\right\|^{q}\right]\leq\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q},

as

{\mathbb{E}}_{\hat{\xi}_{j}}\left[\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{j},\hat{\xi}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1})\right]=\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1}).

Moreover, applying
Lemma

G.4
requires to take
\lambda_{1}\geq 2\max_{j\in[t]}\left\|\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1})\right\|
and, since

\left\|\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1})\right\|\leq\left\|\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{j})\right\|_{\text{op}}\cdot\left\|x_{j}-x_{j-1}\right\|\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Ass.}\!~\text{\ref{ass:L-lipschitz-gradients}}}}{\leq}L_{1}\left\|x_{j}-x_{j-1}\right\|=\gamma L_{1},

then it is enough to have
\lambda_{1}\geq 2\gamma L_{1}
. Setting
G_{1}:=\frac{18\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q}}{\alpha}>0
gives the desired bound.

Bounding
6
.

Similarly to the previous lemma, we set
\widetilde{c}_{1}:=8\lambda_{1}^{2}
and
\widetilde{b}_{1}:=\left(\frac{\widetilde{c}_{1}}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{\widetilde{c}_{1}^{2}}{9}+2\widetilde{G}_{1}}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}
. For the choice
\widetilde{G}_{1}
, we have the bound

\sum_{j=1}^{t}\widetilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\eqref{a26983bb-60b7-49ab-ac0e-a1d026f1c51e-2}}}}{\leq}16\lambda_{1}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{q_{j},\hat{\xi}_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\eqref{74b4e2bb-4754-4dd3-ae90-37ef40362f93-hess}}}}{\leq}16\lambda_{1}^{2}\cdot\frac{18\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q}}{\alpha}=\frac{288\lambda_{1}^{4-q}\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q}}{\alpha},

(where we require
\lambda_{1}\geq 2\gamma L_{1}
). Hence, if we let
\widetilde{G}_{1}:=\frac{288\lambda_{1}^{4-q}\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q}}{\alpha}
this establishes the desired bound.

Bounding
7
.

As we already did in the two last paragraphs, we have

\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 7$}}}}}:=\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}_{\hat{\xi}_{j}}\left[\left\|(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|^{2}\right]\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\eqref{74b4e2bb-4754-4dd3-ae90-37ef40362f93-hess}}}}{\leq}\frac{18\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q}}{\alpha},

as desired.

Bounding
8
.

For this bound, if we assume that
\lambda_{1}\geq 2\gamma L_{1}
then, with probability one we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 8$}}}}}:\!

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{b}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\left\|\omega_{j}^{b}\right\|

\displaystyle{\leq}4\lambda_{1}^{1-q}\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q}\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\lambda_{1}^{1-q}\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q}}{\alpha},

where in (
) we use
Lemma

G.4
(with
{\mathbb{E}}_{\hat{\xi}_{j}}\left[\left.\cdot\,\right|\,q_{j}\right]
), more precisely, for any
j\in[t]
,

\left\|\omega_{j}^{b}\right\|\leq 2^{q}\lambda_{1}^{1-q}\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q}\leq 4\lambda_{1}^{1-q}\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q},

since

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{j},\hat{\xi}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1})-\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1})\right\|^{q}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{j},\hat{\xi}_{j})-\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{j})\right\|_{\text{op}}^{q}\cdot\left\|x_{j}-x_{j-1}\right\|^{q}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Lem.}\!~\text{\ref{lem:tower-property}}}}{=}\gamma^{q}\,\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\nabla^{2}f(\hat{x}_{j},\hat{\xi}_{j})-\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{j})\right\|_{\text{op}}^{q}\,\right|\,\hat{x}_{j}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Ass.}\!~\text{\ref{ass:q-bounded-central-moment-hessian}}}}{\leq}\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q}.

.

For the last bound, we use the triangle inequality, this gives

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 9$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 9$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 9$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 9$}}}}}:\!

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{j=1}4(1-\alpha)^{tj}\omega_{j}^{r}\right\|\leq\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\left\|\omega_{j}^{r}\right\|

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\left\|\nabla^{2}F(\hat{x}_{j})(x_{j}-x_{j-1})-\left[\nabla F(x_{j})-\nabla F(x_{j-1})\right]\right\|

\displaystyle{\leq}\min\left\{2\gamma L_{1},\frac{\gamma^{2}L_{2}}{2}\right\}\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\leq\frac{2\min\left\{\gamma L_{1},\gamma^{2}L_{2}\right\}}{\alpha},

(142)

where in (

90
) and (

), which holds with probability one.

and
\mathbb{P}\left(E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 6$}}}}},t}\right)\geq 1-\delta^{\prime\prime}
hence, by the union bound inequality we have

\mathbb{P}\left(E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 5$}}}}},t}\cap E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 6$}}}}},t}\right)\geq 1-2\delta^{\prime\prime},

and on the event
E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 5$}}}}},t}\cap E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 6$}}}}},t}
we obtain the inequality

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{r}\right\|+\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{b}\right\|+\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}^{u}\right\|

\displaystyle{\leq}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 9$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 9$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 9$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 9$}}}}}+\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 5$}}}}}+\sqrt{2\cdot\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 6$}}}}}+2\cdot\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 7$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 7$}}}}}}+\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[10.44444pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 8$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.40283pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\oval(0.0,0.0)}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 8$}}}}}

\displaystyle{\leq}\frac{2\min\left\{\gamma L_{1},\gamma^{2}L_{2}\right\}}{\alpha}+\frac{22\lambda_{1}}{3}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{2\cdot\frac{176\lambda_{1}^{2}}{3}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}+2\cdot\frac{18\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q}}{\alpha}}+\frac{4\lambda_{1}^{1-q}\gamma^{q}\sigma_{2}^{q}}{\alpha}

\displaystyle{\leq}\frac{2\min\left\{\gamma L_{1},\gamma^{2}L_{2}\right\}}{\alpha}+\lambda_{1}\left(\frac{22}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{176}{3}+\frac{36}{\alpha}\left(\frac{\gamma\sigma_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)^{q}}+\frac{4}{\alpha}\left(\frac{\gamma\sigma_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)^{q}\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle{\leq}\frac{2\min\left\{\gamma L_{1},\gamma^{2}L_{2}\right\}}{\alpha}+\lambda_{1}\left(\frac{22}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{176}{3}+36}+4\right)\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\min\left\{\gamma L_{1},\gamma^{2}L_{2}\right\}}{\alpha}+22\lambda_{1}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}},

where in (
) we use the fact that
\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}\geq 1
and in (
) we use
\lambda_{1}\geq\gamma\sigma_{2}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}
. This proves the lemma.
∎

F.2
Proof of
Theorem

6.1

With
Lemmas

F.3
and
F.4
in our hands, we are now ready to prove the main result of this section, i.e., high-probability convergence guarantees for
Algorithm

3
, i.e.,
clip
-NSGD-MVR
.

Theorem
6.1
.

Under
2.1
,
2.3
and
2.6
, let
T\geq 1
and
\delta\in\mathopen{(}0\mathclose{},1\mathclose{]}
such that
\log\frac{8T}{\delta}\geq 1
and suppose that we choose
g_{0}=0
in
Algorithm

3
and let
\Delta_{1}:=F(x_{0})-F^{\inf}
the initial sub-optimality. Suppose we run
Algorithm

3
using momentum parameter
\alpha=\max\{T^{-\frac{p}{2p-1}},T^{-\frac{pq}{p(2q+1)-2q}}\}
, clipping thresholds
\lambda_{1}=2\gamma\bar{L}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}
and
\lambda_{2}=\max\{4\sqrt{\bar{L}\Delta_{1}},\sigma_{1}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{p}}\}
and with stepsize

\gamma=\mathcal{O}\left(\min\left\{\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{1}}{\bar{L}T}},\alpha\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{1}}{\bar{L}}},\frac{1}{\alpha T\log\frac{T}{\delta}}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{1}}{\bar{L}}},\frac{\Delta_{1}}{\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}T\log\frac{T}{\delta}},\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{1}\alpha^{\frac{1}{q}}}{\bar{L}T\log\frac{T}{\delta}}}\right\}\right).

Then, with probability at least
1-\delta
, the output of
Algorithm

3
satisfies

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|\leq\frac{2\Delta_{1}}{\gamma T},

and, by our choice of parameters, the norm of the gradients converges at the rate

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|=\mathcal{O}\left(\left(\frac{\sqrt{\bar{L}\Delta_{1}}+\sigma_{1}}{T^{\frac{p-1}{2p-1}\wedge\frac{q(p-1)}{p(2q+1)-2q}}}\right)\log\frac{T}{\delta}\right),

with high probability.

Proof.

Let us remind from the proofs of
Lemmas

F.3
and
F.4
that we defined

\displaystyle E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}},t}:\!

\displaystyle=\left\{\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}V_{j}^{t}\right\rvert\leq b_{2}\,\text{ or }\,\sum_{j=1}^{t}\sigma_{j}^{2}>G_{2}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}\right\},

\displaystyle E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}},t}:\!

\displaystyle=\left\{\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}Y_{j}^{t}\right\rvert\leq\widetilde{b}_{2}\,\text{ or }\,\sum_{j=1}^{t}\widetilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}>\widetilde{G}_{2}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}\right\},

\displaystyle E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 5$}}}}},t}:\!

\displaystyle=\left\{\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}W_{j}^{t}\right\rvert\leq b_{1}\,\text{ or }\,\sum_{j=1}^{t}\sigma_{j}^{2}>G_{1}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}\right\},\quad

\displaystyle E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 6$}}}}},t}:\!

\displaystyle=\left\{\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}Z_{j}^{t}\right\rvert\leq\widetilde{b}_{1}\,\text{ or }\,\sum_{j=1}^{t}\widetilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}>\widetilde{G}_{1}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}\right\},

where

c_{1}:=2\lambda_{1},\quad\widetilde{c}_{1}:=8\lambda_{1}^{2},\quad c_{2}:=2\lambda_{2},\quad\widetilde{c}_{2}:=8\lambda_{2}^{2},

G_{1}:=\frac{72\lambda_{1}^{2-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}}{\alpha},\quad\widetilde{G}_{1}:=\frac{1152\lambda_{1}^{4-q}\gamma^{q}\bar{L}^{q}}{\alpha},\quad G_{2}:=\frac{18\lambda_{2}^{2-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{\alpha},\quad\widetilde{G}_{2}:=\frac{288\lambda_{2}^{4-p}\sigma_{1}^{p}}{\alpha},

b_{1}\leq\frac{40\lambda_{1}}{3}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}},\quad\widetilde{b}_{1}\leq\frac{160\lambda_{1}^{2}}{3}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}},\quad b_{2}\leq\frac{22\lambda_{2}}{3}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}},\quad\widetilde{b}_{2}\leq\frac{88\lambda_{2}^{2}}{3}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}},

and we have shown the following bounds:

\mathbb{P}\left(E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}},t}\right)\geq 1-\delta^{\prime},\quad\mathbb{P}\left(E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}},t}\right)\geq 1-\delta^{\prime},\quad\mathbb{P}\left(E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 5$}}}}},t}\right)\geq 1-\delta^{\prime\prime},\quad\mathbb{P}\left(E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 6$}}}}},t}\right)\geq 1-\delta^{\prime\prime},

valid for all
t\in\left\{0,\ldots,T-1\right\}
.

The idea of the proof is based on a technique from previous works in the literature
(
28
;
64
;
48
;
65
)
and proceeds by a mathematical induction. Our goal is to prove that for any
\tau\in\left\{0,\ldots,T-1\right\}
, the event

G_{\tau}:=E_{\tau}\cap E_{1,\tau}\cap E_{2,\tau}\cap E_{5,\tau}\cap E_{6,\tau},

holds with probability at least
1-\frac{\tau\delta}{T}
, where the event
E_{\tau}
is defined as

E_{\tau}:=\bigcap_{t=0}^{\tau}\left\{\gamma\sum_{j=0}^{t}\left\|\nabla F(x_{j})\right\|+\Delta_{t+1}\leq 2\Delta_{1}\right\},

and

E_{1,\tau}:=\bigcap_{t=1}^{\tau}E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}},t},\quad E_{2,\tau}:=\bigcap_{t=1}^{\tau}E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}},t},\quad E_{5,\tau}:=\bigcap_{t=1}^{\tau}E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 5$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 5$}}}}},t},\quad E_{6,\tau}:=\bigcap_{t=1}^{\tau}E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[6.25555pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptscriptstyle 6$}}}}},t}.

Base case.

For the base case
\tau=0
we have
G_{0}=E_{0}=\left\{\Delta_{1}\leq 2\Delta_{1}\right\}
which holds with probability
1=1-\frac{\tau\delta}{T}
since
\tau=0
.

Inductive case.

Now, let us assume that the induction hypothesis holds for
\tau-1
, i.e.,
\mathbb{P}\left(G_{\tau-1}\right)\geq 1-\frac{(\tau-1)\delta}{T}
and we need to prove that is holds for
\tau
also, that is,
\mathbb{P}\left(G_{\tau}\right)\geq 1-\frac{\tau\delta}{T}
. First, let us observe that

E_{\tau-1}\cap E_{1,\tau}\cap E_{2,\tau}\cap E_{5,\tau}\cap E_{6,\tau}=G_{\tau-1}\cap E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 1$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle 2$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\textstyle 6$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[7.15778pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\scriptstyle \displaystyle{\geq}1-\frac{(\tau-1)\delta}{T}-2\delta^{\prime}-2\delta^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=1-\frac{\tau\delta}{T}+\left(\frac{\delta}{T}-2\delta^{\prime}-2\delta^{\prime\prime}\right)

\displaystyle=1-\frac{\tau\delta}{T},

(147)

provided
\delta^{\prime}=\delta^{\prime\prime}=\frac{\delta}{4T}
. Now, given
\tau\in\left\{0,\ldots,T-1\right\}
, on the event
E_{\tau-1}
we have

\Delta_{t}\leq\gamma\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{j})\right\|+\Delta_{t}\leq 2\Delta_{1}

for all integer
1\leq t\leq\tau
and therefore, for all
j\in\left\{0,\ldots,\tau-1\right\}
, we have

\left\|\nabla F(x_{j})\right\|\leq\sqrt{2\bar{L}\Delta_{j}}\leq 2\sqrt{\bar{L}\Delta_{1}},

hence, by our choice of clipping threshold
\lambda_{2}=\max\left\{4\sqrt{\bar{L}\Delta_{1}},\sigma_{1}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{p}}\right\}
we have
\lambda_{2}\geq 2\max_{j\in[t]}\left\|\nabla F(x_{j})\right\|
. Additionally, if we assume the clipping level
\lambda_{1}=2\gamma\bar{L}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}
then, on the event
E_{\tau-1}\cap E_{1,\tau}\cap E_{2,\tau}\cap E_{5,\tau}\cap E_{6,\tau}
, we have

\displaystyle\gamma\sum_{t=0}^{\tau}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|+\Delta_{\tau+1}

\displaystyle{\leq}\Delta_{1}\begin{aligned} &+\frac{2\gamma\sqrt{2\bar{L}\Delta_{1}}}{\alpha}+\frac{\gamma^{2}\bar{L}(\tau+1)}{2}\\
&+2\gamma\alpha\sum_{t=1}^{\tau}\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\theta_{j}\right\|+2\gamma(1-\alpha)\sum_{t=1}^{\tau}\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}(1-\alpha)^{t-j}\omega_{j}\right\|\end{aligned}

\displaystyle{\leq}\Delta_{1}\begin{aligned} &+\frac{2\gamma\sqrt{2\bar{L}\Delta_{1}}}{\alpha}+\frac{\gamma^{2}\bar{L}(\tau+1)}{2}\\
&+44\gamma\alpha\tau\lambda_{2}\log\frac{8T}{\delta}+92\gamma(1-\alpha)\tau\lambda_{1}\log\frac{8T}{\delta},\end{aligned}

and, as
0\leq\tau\leq T-1
then, by our choice of stepsize

\gamma=\min\left\{\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{1}}{2\bar{L}T}},\frac{\alpha}{8}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{1}}{2\bar{L}}},\frac{1}{704\alpha T\log\frac{8T}{\delta}}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{1}}{\bar{L}}},\frac{\Delta_{1}}{176\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}T\log\frac{8T}{\delta}},\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{1}\alpha^{\frac{1}{q}}}{736\bar{L}T\log\frac{8T}{\delta}}}\right\},

we have

\gamma\sum_{t=0}^{\tau}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|+\Delta_{\tau+1}\overset{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\eqref{787efe83-7ee7-4154-be0c-7c0b97faf778}}}}{\leq}\Delta_{1}+\frac{\Delta_{1}}{4}+\frac{\Delta_{1}}{4}+\frac{\Delta_{1}}{4}+\frac{\Delta_{1}}{4}=2\Delta_{1},

therefore we have
E_{\tau}\cap E_{1,\tau}\cap E_{2,\tau}\cap E_{5,\tau}\cap E_{6,\tau}=E_{\tau-1}\cap E_{1,\tau}\cap E_{2,\tau}\cap E_{5,\tau}\cap E_{6,\tau}
which leads to

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(G_{\tau}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left(E_{\tau}\cap E_{1,\tau}\cap E_{2,\tau}\cap E_{5,\tau}\cap E_{6,\tau}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left(E_{\tau-1}\cap E_{1,\tau}\cap E_{2,\tau}\cap E_{5,\tau}\cap E_{6,\tau}\right)

\displaystyle{\geq}1-\frac{\tau\delta}{T},

(149)

as claimed. This achieves the proof of the induction.

Bounding
\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|
in high-probability.

From the previous paragraph, we have for
\tau=T

\mathbb{P}\left(E_{T}\right)\geq\mathbb{P}\left(G_{T}\right)\geq 1-\delta,

hence, with probability at least
1-\delta
we have

\gamma\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|+\Delta_{T}\leq 2\Delta_{1},

which implies the bound

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|\leq\frac{2\Delta_{1}}{\gamma T},

with probability at least
1-\delta
. By our choice of stepsize (
F.2
) we obtain

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|\leq\frac{2\Delta_{1}}{\gamma T}=\mathcal{O}\left(\max\left\{\sqrt{\frac{\bar{L}\Delta_{1}}{T}},\frac{\sqrt{\bar{L}\Delta_{1}}}{\alpha T},\alpha\sqrt{\bar{L}\Delta_{1}}\log\frac{T}{\delta},\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\log\frac{T}{\delta},\sqrt{\frac{\bar{L}\Delta_{1}\log\frac{T}{\delta}}{T\alpha^{\frac{1}{q}}}}\right\}\right),

and choosing
\alpha=\max\left\{T^{-\frac{p}{2p-1}},T^{-\frac{pq}{p(2q+1)-2q}}\right\}
we have

\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}=\max\left\{T^{-\frac{p-1}{2p-1}},T^{-\frac{q(p-1)}{p(2q+1)-2q}}\right\},\,\,\text{ and }\,\,T^{\frac{1}{2}}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2q}}\geq T^{\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\frac{p}{p(2q+1)-2q}\right)}=T^{\frac{q(p-1)}{p(2q+1)-2q}},

and

\alpha T\geq T^{1-\frac{p}{2p-1}}=T^{\frac{p-1}{2p-1}},

thus, assuming
\log\frac{8T}{\delta}\geq 1
we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\Bigg(\max\Bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{\bar{L}\Delta_{1}}{T}},\frac{\sqrt{\bar{L}\Delta_{1}}}{T^{\frac{p-1}{2p-1}}}\log\frac{T}{\delta},\frac{\sqrt{\bar{L}\Delta_{1}}}{T^{\frac{q(p-1)}{p(2q+1)-2q}}}\log\frac{T}{\delta},\frac{\sigma_{1}}{T^{\frac{p-1}{2p-1}}}\log\frac{T}{\delta},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\frac{\sigma_{1}}{T^{\frac{q(p-1)}{p(2q+1)-2q}}}\log\frac{T}{\delta},\frac{\sqrt{\bar{L}\Delta_{1}\log\frac{T}{\delta}}}{T^{\frac{q(p-1)}{p(2q+1)-2q}}}\Bigg\}\Bigg)

\displaystyle{=}\mathcal{O}\left(\left(\frac{\sqrt{\bar{L}\Delta_{1}}+\sigma_{1}}{T^{\frac{p-1}{2p-1}\wedge\frac{q(p-1)}{p(2q+1)-2q}}}\right)\log\frac{T}{\delta}\right)

since
\frac{p-1}{2p-1}\leq\frac{1}{2}
. In (
) we use
\wedge
to denote the minimum between the two exponents
\frac{p-1}{2p-1}
and
\frac{q(p-1)}{p(2q+1)-2q}
.

This concludes the proof of the theorem.
∎

F.3
Proof of
Theorem

F.1

Theorem F.1
.

Under
2.1
,
2.2
,
2.3
and
2.7
, let
T\geq 1
and
\beta\in\mathopen{(}0\mathclose{},1\mathclose{]}
be such that
\log\frac{8T}{\beta}\geq 1
. Let
x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and define
\Delta_{1}:=F(x_{0})-F^{\inf}
.
Suppose that
Algorithm

3
is run with
g_{0}=0
, momentum parameter
\alpha=\max\{T^{-\frac{p}{2p-1}},T^{-\frac{pq}{p(2q+1)-2q}}\}
, clipping thresholds
\lambda_{1}=\max\{2\gamma L_{1},\gamma\delta\alpha^{-\frac{1}{q}}\}
and
\lambda_{2}=\max\{4\sqrt{L_{1}\Delta_{1}},\sigma_{1}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{p}}\}
, and stepsize

\displaystyle\gamma

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\min\left\{\alpha\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{1}}{L_{1}}},\frac{1}{\alpha T\log\frac{T}{\beta}}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{1}}{L_{1}}},\frac{\Delta_{1}}{\sigma_{1}\alpha^{\frac{p-1}{p}}T\log\frac{T}{\beta}},\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{1}\alpha^{\frac{1}{q}}}{\delta T\log\frac{T}{\beta}}},\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{1}}{L_{1}T\log\frac{T}{\beta}}}\right\}\right).

Then, with probability at least
1-\beta
, the output of
Algorithm

3
satisfies

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|\leq\frac{2\Delta_{1}}{\gamma T},

and, by our choice of parameters, the norm of the gradients converges with high probability at the rate

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla F(x_{t})\right\|

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\left(\frac{\sqrt{L_{1}\Delta_{1}}+\sigma_{1}}{T^{\frac{p-1}{2p-1}\wedge\frac{q(p-1)}{p(2q+1)-2q}}}\right)\log\frac{T}{\beta}+\frac{\sqrt{\delta\Delta_{1}}}{T^{\frac{q(p-1)}{p(2q+1)-2q}}}\sqrt{\log\frac{T}{\beta}}\right).

Proof.

\displaystyle=\left\{\left\lvert\sum_{j=1}^{t}Z_{j}^{t}\right\rvert\leq\widetilde{b}_{1}\,\text{ or }\,\sum_{j=1}^{t}\widetilde{\sigma}_{j}^{2}>\widetilde{G}_{1}\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime\prime}}\right\},

where

and we have shown the following bounds:

1-\delta^{\prime},\quad\mathbb{P}\left(E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 1-\delta^{\prime},\quad\mathbb{P}\left(E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 1-\delta^{\prime\prime},\quad\mathbb{P}\left(E_{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.0pt}{\makebox[8.51111pt]{\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(0.0,0.0){\circle{0.0}}}\pic@makebox@{\makebox}{}(0.0,0.0){\put(-0.5,0.0){$\displaystyle 1-\delta^{\prime\prime},

valid for all
